89. 【2610.21470】Cross-Prompt Inference Mitigates Rare-Class Suppression in Imbalanced VLM Finetuning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21470

作者：Emanuel Ben Baruch,Sivan Biham,Alon Shoshan,Yochai Zvik,Avi Ben-Cohen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：class-imbalanced data, zero-shot performance, finetuned on class-imbalanced, rare-class recall, recall

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:When a vision-language model is finetuned on class-imbalanced data, rare-class recall can fall well below the model's own zero-shot performance. In this regime, we uncover a surprising matched-prompt penalty whereby the standard choice of reusing the descriptive finetuning prompt at inference can yield lower rare-class recall than using a different descriptive prompt. We call this inference-time strategy cross-prompt inference. We evaluate diverse VLMs across six datasets and find that cross-prompt inference consistently recovers rare-class recall, with varying trade-offs in frequent-class recall. The effect reflects a train-eval prompt interaction rather than prompt quality alone, and does not arise with non-descriptive prompt changes. It is strongest when imbalance and visual confusability produce substantial rare-class suppression, and is weak or absent when such suppression does not occur. Using Tuning Contribution (TuCo), which decomposes a model's computation into pretrained and finetuning parts, we find that cross-prompt inference reduces reliance on finetuning-induced computation and shifts the model toward its pretrained component. Reducing that finetuning contribution also substantially reduces the cross-prompt advantage. These results show that rare-class knowledge suppressed by imbalanced finetuning can remain accessible under a different inference prompt.

97. 【2610.21409】CLAD: Keypoint Matching Using Global Line Correspondences

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21409

作者：Masaki Satoh

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

备注： 5 pages, 2 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Traditional keypoint descriptors, such as SIFT (Scale-Invariant Feature Transform) and ORB (Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF), are computed from local image patches. They may be unreliable in scenes with repetitive patterns or weak textures, where patches at different locations can look similar. We propose the Contextual Line-Assisted Descriptor (CLAD), a keypoint matching method that addresses this problem by using global geometric context from line correspondences. CLAD represents each keypoint with a vector whose elements indicate its proximity to matched lines. It can therefore use the keypoint's spatial relationships to the lines in the scene. We use HOME (Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema) to detect both clear and weak linear structures and to establish enough line-to-keypoint associations to represent this context. Evaluations on the TUM RGB-D SLAM dataset and outdoor video sequences show that CLAD provides geometrically consistent matches that complement those obtained with traditional local descriptors. In repetitive scenes, where local descriptors may not distinguish similar patches, CLAD uses global structure to improve matching reliability. The method is computationally lightweight and practical to implement.

98. 【2610.21380】Perception or Reasoning? Diagnosing and Tackling Reinforcement Learning Bottlenecks in VLMs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21380

作者：Zeyuan Yang,Zhuohao Chen,Jiaben Chen,Yao Zhang,Maohao Shen,Chuang Gan

关键词：Reinforcement learning, brought strong gains, visual tasks remain, remain less consistent, language model reasoning

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning has brought strong gains to language model reasoning, but its improvements on visual tasks remain less consistent. One difficulty is that visual task failures can arise from different sources: the same benchmark may be limited by perception for one model and by reasoning for another. We introduce Perception and Reasoning Attribution (PARA), which measures the remaining perceptual and reasoning headroom of each model--benchmark pair by comparing how many current errors are recovered with more accurate visual information versus a stronger reasoner. Across 4 vision-language models and 17 multimodal benchmarks, PARA broadly agrees with the expected characteristics of existing benchmarks while revealing substantial model-specific variation. More importantly, it shows that RL gains become smaller as perceptual headroom increases, but larger when reasoning explains more of the recoverable errors, suggesting that current RL exploits reasoning headroom more effectively than perceptual headroom. Motivated by this gap, we analyze how existing RL objectives affect visual perception and find that reward- and token-level signals influence visual attention only indirectly. We therefore propose GRAD, a group-relative attention distillation objective that uses the best rollout in each group to directly guide the visual attention of weaker rollouts. GRAD consistently improves performance across two model scales and seven benchmarks, and further complements existing RL methods.

99. 【2610.21367】SATOR: Backpropagation-Free 3D Test-Time Adaptation via Sample-Adaptive Token Routing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21367

作者：Inseok Kong,Geunyoung Jung,Jiyoung Jung

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：unlabeled test data, Test-time adaptation, mitigates the performance, distribution shifts, performance degradation

备注： Accepted by WACV 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Test-time adaptation (TTA) mitigates the performance degradation of source-pretrained models on unlabeled test data under distribution shifts. Recent backpropagation-free token-purging methods address 3D point cloud distribution shifts by filtering unreliable tokens. However, their reliance on predefined purge-size candidates requires multiple forward passes per sample and increases inference cost. We propose SATOR (Sample-Adaptive TOken Routing), a source-free and backpropagation-free 3D TTA framework that eliminates this search by deriving both the partitioning threshold and routing decision directly from each sample. SATOR computes token abnormality scores from complementary attention and geometric relation graphs. It then derives a sample-specific partition into retained and pruned tokens via Otsu-based separability, without evaluating candidate sizes. Keep-ratio-calibrated routing then selects the pruned prediction only when its entropy, penalized by the keep ratio, is lower than that of the original prediction. Experiments on ModelNet40-C, ScanObjectNN-C, and ShapeNet-C demonstrate that SATOR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on three 3D corruption benchmarks.

100. 【2610.21364】CoDeFont: One-Shot Font Generation via Multi-Scale Factor-Specific Representation Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21364

作者：Rejoy Chakraborty,Prasun Roy,Saumik Bhattacharya,Umapada Pal

备注： 10 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:One-shot Font Generation (OFG) aims to synthesize a target glyph by transferring the stylistic characteristics of a single reference glyph while preserving the structure of the target character. However, accurately capturing font style from a single reference image remains challenging due to the limited stylistic information available in a single glyph, and this challenge is further amplified for complex scripts. We propose CoDeFont, a script-agnostic OFG framework that addresses this challenge through multi-level style-content factorization and progressive style-content reconciliation. CoDeFont independently aggregates complementary style and structural information across multiple spatial levels to obtain factor-specific representations, while a contrastive objective explicitly encourages separation between style and content embeddings. During reconstruction, the disentangled representations are progressively injected through multi-level style conditioning, enabling stylistic information to be incorporated at different decoding resolutions while preserving the underlying glyph structure. We evaluate CoDeFont on structurally distinct Bengali and Latin scripts across seen and unseen font styles. Extensive quantitative and qualitative analyses demonstrate consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed framework for OFG across diverse writing systems.

Abstract:Generative low-light enhancers recover plausible texture but may hallucinate structure; restoration networks preserve structure but can leave regions under-enhanced, and neither advantage is spatially uniform. Therefore, we formulate selective generative enhancement as a decision problem: determine where overriding a frozen base expert is beneficial and refuse to switch when the evidence is insufficient. We propose VetoNet, an auditable regional routing framework centered on a certified veto. It first measures the complementary strengths and routing direction of a frozen restoration generative pair, then predicts the utility of regional overrides from deployment-available signals alone and admits an override only when a distribution-free harm-budget certificate says the perceptual (LPIPS) risk budget allows it. Experiments across multiple expert pairs and domains show that VetoNet adapts its routing behavior to the strength and reliability of the available evidence. When expert complementarity is sufficiently predictable, it achieves statistically significant certified gains; when the relative strengths of the experts change, it correspondingly reverses the routing direction; and when routing evidence is weak or affected by cross-domain shift, it conservatively vetoes switching.

103. 【2610.21332】Semantic Latent Alignment for Flow Matching with Invertible Bi-Lipschitz Adapters

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21332

作者：Felix Nützel,Mischa Dombrowski,Luca Hagen,Bernhard Kainz

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：latent spaces trade, domains or tasks, spaces trade compression, improve downstream generation, complicates adapting

备注： 17 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pretrained latent spaces trade compression, reconstruction, and the learnability of the coordinates a downstream generator has to work in, which complicates adapting these spaces to different domains or tasks: continued training is expensive, and without a new alignment objective it often fails to improve downstream generation. We instead leave the encoder and decoder fixed and change only the coordinates between them, using an invertible bi-Lipschitz adapter aligned offline to a semantic teacher and frozen during class-conditional flow matching. Invertibility preserves compatibility with the pretrained autoencoder, while the bi-Lipschitz constraint limits how far alignment can distort its latent geometry. This provides a simple way to improve semantic organization while retaining the compression and reconstruction properties of the original latent space. On two medical imaging datasets, constrained latent adaptation improves feature metrics, seed stability and counterfactual edit strength, while remaining competitive on downstream classification. In contrast, unconstrained adaptation can become highly anisotropic and sensitive despite remaining invertible, highlighting the importance of controlling geometric distortion. Our results suggest that carefully constrained post-hoc adaptation offers a parameter-efficient and tuning-free alternative to jointly modifying the representation during generative training.

105. 【2610.21325】CertEx: Exact Visual Retrieval at Scale by Certified Exclusion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21325

作者：Dongfang Zhao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：underlies visual retrieval, embeddings underlies visual, LLM agents, memory of LLM, near-duplicate detection

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Exact $k$-nearest-neighbor (kNN) search over image descriptors and embeddings underlies visual retrieval, near-duplicate detection, the evaluation of learned representations, and increasingly the memory of LLM agents. However, exhaustive search computes every distance at full precision, whereas approximate indexes return recall below one. We observe that exact search only needs a proof that each excluded vector cannot be a neighbor. Because such a proof only has to be conservative, it can be checked in low precision, much as the retina examines the whole scene at low resolution. We call this principle certified exclusion and realize it in an exact kNN method for GPUs, which we call \certex. Specifically, \certex\ stores a lower-bounding projection of each vector in half precision and folds a rounding certificate into the same tensor-core dot product, so that only the few vectors that the test keeps need exact distances. We prove that \certex\ returns the exact kNN of every query. On sixteen datasets with up to 100M vectors, \certex\ is exact for every query at 7.1$\times$ to 18.0$\times$ the throughput of the fastest cuVS search on one H100 and outperforms quantized and graph indexes whenever every query must be exact.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Most vision-language models (VLMs) treat the vision encoder as a static feature extractor: it encodes an image once, independently of the question, and leaves subsequent reasoning to the language model. Yet different questions may require different visual operations and processing depths. Our findings suggest that pretrained vision encoders can contribute to reasoning through task-directed, iterative visual computation.

Abstract:Although Graph Neural Networks are widely used in various domains, they remained mostly unexplored in computer vision. Convolutional Neural Networks and Vision Transformers were leading the field, until the quite recent introduction of Vision Graphs (ViG) that treat images as a set of nodes and apply graph convolution on their k-NN graphs. 111. 【2610.21302】Dusting the Phantom: Gaussian Allocation for Fading Dynamic Content

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21302

作者：Minhyeok Roh,Minseo Lee,Jun Young Jeong,Byeonghyeon Lee,Lucas Yunkyu Lee,Jaesik Park,Eunbyung Park

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：video increasingly practical, real-time volumetric video, volumetric video increasingly, made high-quality, real-time volumetric

备注： 21 pages, 14 figures. Project page: [this https URL](https://minhyeok2001.github.io/Dusting-the-Phantom/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dynamic Gaussian representations have made high-quality, real-time volumetric video increasingly practical, yet challenging motion can still break their reconstruction: small fast-moving objects may disappear, and fine textures in highly dynamic regions are often lost. In particular, simply training longer or using more Gaussians may still fail to recover small, fast-moving objects. We argue that the key bottleneck is not representational capacity itself, but where and when Gaussian primitives are allocated. Existing allocation strategies rely on current Gaussian locations and often overlook the spatiotemporal destinations of relocated Gaussians. To address these limitations, we introduce two complementary strategies. First, Seed Injection identifies under-reconstructed dynamic regions using temporal appearance changes and reconstruction errors, and initializes new Gaussians at geometrically plausible 3D locations through multi-view observations, independent of existing Gaussian locations. Second, Temporal Biased Relocation uses time-resolved optimization signals to guide the spatiotemporal placement of relocated Gaussians. Both strategies maintain a fixed Gaussian budget without modifying the underlying representation. Experiments across multiple dynamic-scene datasets demonstrate improved reconstruction quality, particularly in highly dynamic regions, recovering small, rapidly moving objects and fine-grained appearance details.

Abstract:Training-free object removal commonly redirects self-attention away from the target region, but on few-step distilled diffusion models this often leaves object-shaped residuals. We identify a temporal mismatch: a structured prediction forms inside the mask at the first forward, and the short trajectory offers few later chances to revise it, while attention rerouting acts mainly in later steps. Abstract:Multi-teacher on-policy distillation enables rectified-flow models with diffusion-transformer architectures to combine task-specific capabilities within a single student. 120. 【2610.21215】DLOSAC: Joint Spatial-Geometric Image Estimation via Delaunay-Guided Local Optimization RANSAC

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21215

作者：Yu Liu,Meiqi Zhou,Zipei Fan,Guanyuan Feng,Xuan Song

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Reliable image geometric, noisy matching points, Reliable image, addressed using RANSAC, noisy matching

备注： Accepted at the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reliable image geometric estimation from noisy matching points is typically addressed using RANSAC, often combined with local optimization to refine inliers and model parameters. However, existing paradigms are predominantly residual-centric; even heuristic attempts to enforce spatial uniformity suffer from a sequential, two-stage nature that inherently decouples distribution optimization from geometric refinement, preventing an effective trade-off. In this paper, we propose DLOSAC, a local optimization framework that jointly incorporates both geometric consistency and spatial distribution information. We begin by constructing a Delaunay triangulation over candidate inliers to explicitly encode neighborhood topology. The geometric module introduces an edge-based consistency metric to evaluate geometric errors, while the spatial module derives distribution descriptors from triangle areas and edge attributes to quantify the clustering of inliers. Finally, we combine edge-based geometric constraints, spatial distribution regularization, and traditional point-level geometric constraints to improve the quality of geometric parameters. Extensive experiments on six publicly available real-world datasets, covering three types of geometric estimation tasks, achieve state-of-the-art performance.

121. 【2610.21207】SpaceCache: Intermediate Feature Reuse for Efficient Semantic Segmentation on Point Cloud Streams

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21207

作者：Dongheon Yang,Beomseok Kang,Jae-Joon Kim

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：existing segmentation networks, segmentation networks process, frames largely overlap, consecutive frames largely, driving and robotics

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Point-cloud streams are central to autonomous driving and robotics, yet existing segmentation networks process every frame from scratch, although consecutive frames largely overlap. We observe that the intermediate features of point-cloud transformers remain semantically consistent across frames for the same spatial region, especially in deep stages. Building on this, we present SpaceCache, a training-free feature cache that reuses these features across frames. Tokens of previously observed regions skip a sequence of transformer blocks and take their cached features, only the remaining tokens are computed, and the full token layout is restored for dense prediction. Lightweight cache management keeps reused features reliable by limiting each entry's age and evicting low-confidence entries. Unlike token merging, SpaceCache selects tokens at negligible cost and runs its cache operations once per encoder stage. On both outdoor and indoor benchmarks, such as nuScenes, Waymo, and ScanNet, with two transformer backbones of Utonia and LitePT-B, SpaceCache achieves up to 1.65x speedup with less than 1-point mIoU drop and consistently outperforms token-merging methods.

122. 【2610.21202】UltraDiff: Transferring High-Fidelity Priors to Compressed Latent Spaces for High-Resolution Image Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21202

作者：Jingjing Ren,Haitian Zheng,Haoyu Chen,Connelly Barnes,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Zhe Lin,Lei Zhu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：High-resolution synthesis, Latent Diffusion Models, faces a fundamental, Latent Diffusion, fundamental trade-off

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:High-resolution synthesis with Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) faces a fundamental trade-off between computational cost and generative fidelity. This trade off is largely governed by the latent compression factor f (e.g. f8 denotes f=8 and f32 f=32). While aggressive compression (f32) significantly improves efficiency, it suffers from severe information loss and ambiguity compared to standard f8 models. We propose UltraDiff, a framework that reformulates this challenge as joint latent diffusion learning followed by marginal score distillation. We introduce MixTeacher, a dual branch DiT that models the joint f8-f32 distribution, utilizing shared attention to resolve the ambiguity of the compressed space with high fidelity f8 priors. Crucially, we propose vector field distillation in the native f32 space. Theoretically, this compels the student to learn the conditional expectation of the teacher trajectory, effectively filtering out compression artifacts. Across the evaluated 1K to 4K settings, UltraDiff approaches f8 level quality while preserving f32 efficiency. With 20 sampling steps on a single A100 GPU, it generates a 2K image in 6.2 seconds over 40 times faster than our f8 baseline.

123. 【2610.21190】ControlFlow3D: Distilling Multi-View Knowledge into Latent Flow Matching for Point Cloud Upsampling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21190

作者：Yuang Liu,Zhi Zuo,Zhengkai Zhao,Lirui Zhang,Pan Gao,Hao Feng,Zhengzhe Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Point cloud upsampling, remains challenging due, Point cloud, under-constrained observations, applications but remains

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Point cloud upsampling is essential for 3D applications but remains challenging due to sparse, under-constrained observations. Multi-view images can resolve geometric ambiguity, but existing multi-modal methods do not fully exploit visual cues. We propose ControlFlow3D, a framework that distills knowledge from pretrained foundation models into a lightweight generation network during training, enabling strong performance without multi-view images available at test time. Specifically, our method performs conditional flow matching in a structured latent space defined by a frozen 3D encoder. Multi-view image tokens and 3D latent tokens are jointly clustered into shared prototypes via unsupervised soft KMeans; the resulting prototypes guide the flow trajectory through velocity control and are aligned by a consistency loss that progressively transfers image knowledge into the base network. A Mamba-based geometry decoder then maps latent features to dense coordinates with linear complexity. We also construct ShapeNetPU, a 35K-object multi-modal benchmark that is 31 times larger than prior datasets. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and its strong generalization capability.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21159

Abstract:On-screen text can describe what a video shows, or it can frame what the video is supposed to mean. 126. 【2610.21129】Sparse Attention for Video Generation Acceleration via Growing Sparsity and Reduced Search

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21129

作者：Yun Dai,Yuyun Chen,Jiarui Wen,Huiping Zhuang,Cen Chen,Ziqian Zeng

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diffusion Transformers, high inference cost, inference cost due, dominate video generation, dominate video

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) dominate video generation but suffer from high inference cost due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Sparse attention mitigates this by skipping unnecessary computation in the attention matrix, but existing methods apply a fixed sparsity ratio throughout denoising and struggle to accurately determine which computations to skip. To address these limitations, we analyze attention dynamics through attention entropy and mean attention distance, and find that attention becomes increasingly concentrated across denoising steps. We further observe that blocks selected early continue to capture a large fraction of the total attention weight at later steps. These findings suggest two acceleration opportunities: the sparsity ratio should grow as denoising progresses, and earlier search results can be reused to reduce the search space. Based on these findings, we propose Growing Sparsity and Reduced Search (GSRS), a training-free framework that retains more computation early and progressively reduces it later, while restricting subsequent searches to an early candidate set to reduce search overhead. GSRS adopts Top-p selection for adaptive sparsity control, supported by a precise evaluation scheme that computes exact attention weights within the FlashAttention computation at negligible overhead. Experiments on HunyuanVideo and Wan 2.1 validate that GSRS achieves 1.55$\times$--2.21$\times$ speedup while maintaining generation quality comparable to full attention.

127. 【2610.21121】ResStreamGS: Residual-Sensitive Dual-Stream Gaussian Splatting for Mobile Volumetric Video Streaming

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21121

作者：Haoran Liu,Di Wu,Wang Luo,Hengyuan Na,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan

类目：Graphics (cs.GR); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 11 pages,9 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Real-time mobile volumetric video enables immersive free-viewpoint experiences, but delivering high-quality 3D content under constrained bandwidth and computation remains challenging. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) systems, such as V$^3$, enable efficient mobile streaming by encoding time-varying Gaussian attributes with commodity video codecs and hardware decoding. However, existing pipelines treat all Gaussians as frame-varying signals, which is poorly suited to full-scene content where large portions remain temporally stable. Our empirical study shows that frame-wise adaptation and video coding of all Gaussians cause quality degradation in complex full-scene content, particularly in temporally stable regions. To address this issue, we propose ResStreamGS, a residual-sensitive dual-stream framework that processes Gaussians according to their temporal behavior. ResStreamGS identifies residual-sensitive primitives through lightweight probe adaptation and reuses stable Gaussians within each Group of Pictures (GOP). 128. 【2610.21114】Spectral-Spatial Interpretation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21114

作者：Haotian Ma,Ruqi Yang,Philip Townsend

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Post-hoc image explanations, Post-hoc image, HLS Burn Scar, SSI, image explanations

备注： 24 pages, 4 figures. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (Poster)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Post-hoc image explanations usually show where a model looks using spatial heatmaps or indicate which channels matter using global channel scores. These separate views are limiting for multispectral data because channel relevance is often spatially localized and physically meaningful. We introduce Spectral-Spatial Interpretation (SSI), a full-grid attribution method over position-channel players (g,c). For each spatial position, SSI fixes the observed surrounding context, defines a conditional game over the channels at that position, and applies the Shapley value to this local game. The result is a full spectral-spatial attribution matrix rather than a single heatmap or a global channel ranking. SSI has a clear local interpretation. It satisfies Shapley fairness within each position, and aggregating its channel attributions exactly recovers the position-level contextual contrast obtained by replacing that position with the baseline. SSI also differs from context-averaged Owen-style hierarchical accounting, which uses the same position-channel grouping but averages over external spatial coalitions. On HLS Burn Scar, SSI outperforms an MC-Owen estimator across retained attribution metrics while using only about 6% of its evaluation budget. Across CUB-200, EuroSAT, and HLS Burn Scar under both per-channel mean and zero baselines, SSI achieves the best joint deletion and insertion AUC over dense, flat, and rank-1 baselines, yields the strongest spectral hit rates and channel deletion, and leads spatial localization on both annotated datasets. Code is available at this https URL.

129. 【2610.21095】Can World and Action Modeling Benefit Each Other under Unified Causal Training?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21095

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://selen-suyue.github.io/OneWMNet)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World and action modeling are closely related, yet their reciprocal benefits under unified causal training remain unclear. We introduce OneWM, a simple framework for unified causal training that treats actions as both conditions for world prediction and targets for policy learning within a shared video-action architecture. Using this framework, we investigate whether learning forward dynamics conditioned on specified actions improves action learning, and whether learning to generate actions in turn improves action-conditioned world modeling. We use LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa365 as embodied testbeds to evaluate both policy learning and world modeling, complemented by real-world policy experiments. Learning the visual consequences of specified actions improves policy performance and generalization across changing scenes. In turn, action learning improves multi-view world-prediction fidelity under the same architecture. Abstract:We present RoboCloth, a dataset of 500 real cloth materials captured under flexible, calibrated camera and lighting configurations, where both are robotically controlled during acquisition. Each material is imaged in approximately 580 high-dynamic range frames spanning a wide view-light hemisphere; camera poses are refined via structure-from-motion and aligned to the robot frame, yielding per-material sparse surface geometry and structured per-point reflectance observations ready for neural material training. We demonstrate the value of this data through a two-stage neural reconstruction pipeline: Stage 1 trains a shared MLP decoder on our corpus to learn cloth material priors; Stage 2 then freezes the decoder and fits a dense 2048x2048 latent texture to each target material. The dataset, calibration files, observation tensors, and train/test splits are released under CC BY 4.0 on the Hugging Face Hub.

131. 【2610.21087】SIEVE: Overcoming Topological Obstruction in Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21087

作者：Jongann Lee,Sun Woo Park,Yun Young Choi

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：cloud masked autoencoding, point cloud masked, identify a theoretical, theoretical incompatibility, masked autoencoding

备注： The Fortieth Annual Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS) 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We identify a theoretical incompatibility between 3D point cloud masked autoencoding and SE(3)-equivariant networks. We prove that standard masking triggers a topological collapse, effectively forcing the model to hallucinate orientation without a reference frame. To resolve this, we introduce SIEVE (Scalar Invariant Extraction Via Equivariance), an SE(3)-equivariant self-supervised framework that compresses point cloud geometry into invariant scalar embeddings. At its core is the spherical projection mask, which preserves global pose while obfuscating local semantics, sidestepping the obstruction by construction. Experiments confirm that SIEVE circumvents the predicted collapse where constant and noise masks fail. On downstream point cloud tasks, the resulting embeddings improve over coordinate-based baselines and hand-crafted geometric descriptors, with the largest gains on generalization to unseen shape categories.

134. 【2610.21007】Mind the Tail: Data Selection Through Sparse Concepts

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21007

作者：Ziheng Zhang,Neha Hulkund,Colin Lee,Zheda Mai,Hui Shen,Ying Ding,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Sara Beery,Wei-Lun Chao,Jianyang Gu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Data selection reallocates, selection reallocates training, reallocates training support, reallocates training, including objects

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://imageomics.github.io/mind-the-tail/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Data selection reallocates training support across visual concepts, including objects, attributes, color, texture, and backgrounds. Different allocation strategies may produce varied downstream model behavior, especially for tail concepts supported by only a few samples. Discarding them can hurt test-time robustness, while overemphasizing samples lying on category boundaries may introduce unstable training signals. This work explicitly investigates this relationship across fine-grained, spurious-correlation, and general image classification, as well as multimodal pretraining. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to extract concept activations from individual images. During selection, we accumulate the dataset-level concept support for each added sample. Applying diminishing returns to this distribution down-weights well-represented concepts and shifts the focus to the distribution tail. We then incorporate local support to find samples that reliably strengthen under-supported concepts. Empirical results show consistent improvements over strong data selection baselines across all benchmarks. We also reveal the connection between concepts our method enhances and the induced behavioral changes, including improved robustness against out-of-distribution and spurious contexts. This work establishes concept support, beyond individual sample importance, as a useful principle for data-efficient and robust learning.

135. 【2610.20999】EigDiff: Eigensample Diffusion - or how to significantly reduce training dataset size with marginal performance variation?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20999

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion models are notoriously expensive to train, requiring large-scale datasets to learn how to gradually reverse the noise from images over multiple small denoising steps. Current approaches focus on distilling the number of diffusion steps, neglecting potential redundancies of the training data. In this paper, we explore the potential of harnessing eigensamples to train diffusion models on significantly smaller datasets, while preserving model performance. To mitigate the sensitivity to affine transformations of eigenimages, we harness the latent space of a pre-trained CLIP model. We next compute latent eigenvectors by performing principal component analysis (PCA) in CLIP space. Further, we employ eigenvectors as reference points to guide the selection of a representative training set comprising comprising both exemplars (images whose embeddings are closest to eigenvectors) and diverse images. Our comprehensive experiments with different diffusion models applied on multiple datasets indicate that our approach (EigDiff) can reduce training data by up to 14x and accelerate subset selection by up to 1607x, with marginal performance differences.

136. 【2610.20990】DeSpatial: The devil of Agentic Spatial Reasoning Lies in 3D Itself

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20990

作者：Yifei Liu,Xiaolong Zhou,Ziyang Gong,Yifan Zhou,Yuanyuan Gao,Yuning Gong,Yuchen Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Xue Yang,Xiao Sun,Zhihang Zhong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：fragmented object identities, access to reconstruction, predictions and fragmented, identities can prevent, Spatial

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spatial agents have access to reconstruction and segmentation tools, yet inconsistent predictions and fragmented object identities can prevent their outputs from becoming reliable 3D scene information. A simple oracle experiment exposes this perception bottleneck: replacing tool-predicted perception with ground-truth 3D bounding boxes raises an agent to nearly full score across five object-centric VSI-Bench tasks, while retaining the same reasoning tools. Motivated by this finding, we believe the devil of spatial reasoning lies in 3D itself, and introduce DeSpatial(Devil of Spatial), which explicitly decomposes spatial understanding into perception and reasoning. DeSpatial has a shared 3D perception tool to reconcile independent perception outputs into a unified, metric, gravity-aligned 3D scene. This perception tool has two core components: a) a joint segmentation--depth co-refinement module that consolidates masks of the same object and removes projection artifacts caused by inconsistent segmentation and depth within a single frame; b) a cross-view instance fusion module that consolidate voxel-overlapping instances but not allowed to merge same-frame instances. This fusion module additionally fuses spatially disjoint fragments caused by insufficient multi-view observations, by checking their 3D structure and source mask appearance. After receiving the complete and unified 3D scene information, the agent chooses reasoning or computational tools to solve the spatial problem. DeSpatial achieves leading performance on VSI-Bench, MindCube-Tiny, and ViewSpatial, with additional long-video results on VSI-SUPER, revealing the bottleneck of spatial understanding lies in a precise 3D perception itself.

137. 【2610.20989】Do As I Do, Not As I Say: On-Policy Distillation Inherits the Teacher in Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20989

作者：Frank F. Yang,Kefei Yan,Yiren Wang,Yiyang Wang

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Improving the reasoning, vision-language models, stronger models, OPD, SFT

备注： 27 pages, 7 figures, 21 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/ffy208/opd-inherits-teacher-vlm)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Improving the reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) often relies on post-training with supervision from stronger models. Two common strategies are supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which trains on teacher-generated demonstrations, and on-policy distillation (OPD), which provides teacher feedback on trajectories generated by the student itself. While OPD can be more data-efficient by supervising student-generated trajectories, it is unclear how the teacher's supervision at each state affects the student's learned behavior. To study this question, we conduct a controlled comparison of OPD and filtered SFT across data budgets and tasks where the teacher exhibits different levels of reliability, and analyze how these differences shape student accuracy and completion behavior. Across the two tasks, we find that OPD is effective only when the teacher remains reliable on student-generated trajectories. On ChartQA, where the teacher completes 99% of its solutions, OPD trained on only 10% of the data comes within 1.4 accuracy points of full-data SFT. In contrast, on Geometry3K, where the teacher fails to complete 22% of its solutions, OPD underperforms SFT by 7.9 points, with most of the gap explained by incomplete responses. More strikingly, starting OPD from an SFT student reduces its completion rate from 76% to 62%, effectively erasing the completion behavior learned through SFT. Filtering teacher demonstrations for completeness alone recovers much of SFT's advantage. These results show that on-policy supervision can transfer not only teacher knowledge but also persistent failure modes on student-visited states. Code is available at this https URL.

138. 【2610.20973】PiCo: Physics-Informed Concept Probing for Geo-Foundation Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20973

作者：Yihang She,Andrew Blake,Jianbo Qi,Srinivasan Keshav

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：task predictive performance, Geo-foundation models, downstream task predictive, typically evaluated, task predictive

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Geo-foundation models (GFMs) are typically evaluated by downstream task predictive performance, revealing little about which physical features they are sensitive to or use in prediction. This limits developers' ability to test hypotheses about model behaviour and users' ability to select models for new tasks. We propose Physics-Informed Concept Probing (PiCo), a post-hoc framework for explaining GFM predictions. PiCo identifies concept vectors in a GFM's embedding space by generating synthetic multispectral satellite observations from a 3D radiative transfer model coupled with a game engine. These vectors reveal physical sensitivities that inform domain-based hypotheses about downstream performance. Projecting embeddings onto these concept vectors produces low-dimensional representations for quantifying concept importance in making predictions. We evaluate PiCo on three GFMs with different input modalities using seven physical concepts covering forest leaf optics and canopy structure. In ground-cover estimation, RGB restriction reduces dry-matter sensitivity and disproportionately degrades predictions for dry vegetation and bare ground, supporting a hypothesis derived from sensitivity analysis. For hierarchical forest type and tree species classification, concept representations retain 70.0-93.8% of full-embedding accuracy. Models capture similar physical contrasts between classes, such as structural differences between broadleaf forest and cleared ground and leaf-water-content differences between oak and beech, yet differ in the strength of these contrasts and the concepts driving classification. PiCo thus provides a physical basis for hypothesis testing and model assessment, with potential applicability beyond Earth observation wherever forward models enable controlled simulation and pretrained embeddings are accessible for probing.

139. 【2610.20965】MSP: Modality Self-Play for Training Multimodal LLMs Without Paired Cross-Modal Data

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20965

作者：Robert Moseley,Md Kaykobad Reza,Ameya Patil,Edward Ayrapetian,Salman Asif

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：achieved remarkable progress, Large multimodal models, Large multimodal, scaling model capacity, achieved remarkable

备注： 21 pages, 4 figures, 16 tables. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large multimodal models have achieved remarkable progress by scaling model capacity and training on massive paired multimodal data. However, this paradigm introduces a fundamental bottleneck: aligned multimodal datasets are expensive to curate, difficult to scale across diverse modality combinations, and increasingly constrain further progress. We introduce Modality Self-Play (MSP), a training framework for multimodal foundation models based on proxy-mediated modality composition. Instead of relying on paired cross-modal data, MSP uses anchor-supporting proxy textual descriptions of unseen modalities as semantic bridges during training. Given an observed modality (e.g., image or audio), the model generates proxy descriptions for complementary unseen modalities, which are then encoded and aligned with the observed modality through learned bridges, enabling cross-modal interaction without real paired non-text supervision. Our central hypothesis is that proxy text, combined with contrastively trained pretrained encoders, provides sufficient semantic structure for multimodal composition to emerge. We show that MSP enables zero-shot modality composition: models trained only with a real anchor modality and accompanying proxy descriptions can integrate and reason over multiple modalities at inference time despite never observing real paired non-text data during training. Empirically, MSP achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on multiple multimodal benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate that learned bridges improve alignment, while anchor-supporting proxy descriptions enable effective cross-modal composition. MSP removes the need for real paired non-text (e.g., audio-vision) supervision, while continuing to rely on modality-text pairs, pretrained contrastive encoders, and modality-distribution bridge calibration.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20958

作者：Dara Molloy,Terry Wu,Wonju Lee,Abhilash Nand Kumar,David Adley,Conor Ferguson,Hesam Rabeti

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Autonomous mobile robots, point-feature visual odometry, production facilities operate, defeat point-feature visual, Autonomous mobile

备注： 8 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Autonomous mobile robots in warehouses and production facilities operate in precisely the environments that defeat point-feature visual odometry: featureless aisles, uniform walls, repetitive racking and specular floors. Such environments are, however, rich in straight-edge structure: rack uprights, door frames, wall junctions and cabinet outlines present high-contrast linear edges stable across wide viewpoint changes. A line-feature extension to cuVSLAM, a CUDA-accelerated visual-SLAM system, is presented that recovers this signal. Lines are parameterised as Plücker coordinates for geometric transport and as the minimal 4-DOF orthonormal representation of Bartoli and Sturm for optimisation; a Cauchy-robust point-to-line residual with analytic Jacobians is folded directly into cuVSLAM's per-frame Gauss-Newton pose solve, and the same line state is jointly refined in the Schur-complement sliding-window bundle adjustment. An adaptive information weight causes lines to assume more of the estimation burden exactly as point features become scarce, and the extension adds residual blocks to existing solvers rather than introducing a second estimator, reducing exactly to the unmodified point pipeline when disabled. Evaluated in RGB-D mode under SE(3) Umeyama alignment with no scale correction, line features reduce mean absolute trajectory error by 38.5% over the 22 sequences of the TUM RGB-D Freiburg3 collection and by 30.1% over the 8 Replica room and office sequences, where median translation error falls by 38.9% and median rotation error by 66.4%, with zero tracking failures in any configuration tested.

142. 【2610.20917】Decision-FLIP: Budget-Free Structured Pruning for Gaussian Occupancy Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20917

作者：Jingxing Li,Anurag Paul,Subhasis Das,Cindy Xu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Occupancy predictors rely, Occupancy predictors, Gaussians or learned, fixed by hand, large persistent banks

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Occupancy predictors rely on large persistent banks of Gaussians or learned queries whose size is fixed by hand. Common pruning proxies, such as opacity, activation frequency, or parameter magnitude, do not measure whether removing a row changes the model's voxel decisions. We introduce Decision-FLIP, a budget-free compilation framework whose scoring and structure selection need only unlabeled calibration inputs. Exact Mean-FLIP scores each persistent row by the number of voxel argmax decisions that change when the row is removed from the readout; a guarded Otsu split of the log-score distribution selects the survivor count without a target and abstains when the split is unsupported; and the survivors are physically compiled into a smaller checkpoint, so camera sampling, attention, and aggregation shrink with the bank. For streaming banks, scores are integrated over each row's lifecycle through warp, death, and refill. Across four Gaussian-bank model-dataset settings and one persistent-query transfer, the automatically selected cuts remove 12-79% of eligible rows and cut bank/query-dependent latency by 7-45% across settings with component profiles, with whole-model savings of up to 8.0% on the primary endpoints bounded by fixed-cost image, voxel, and sensor modules and peak-memory savings of up to 16.6%. Under matched one-epoch recovery controls, semantic mIoU changes by at most 0.18 point. Same-$K$ controls show that the ranking, not only the bank size, matters: random removal collapses GaussianFormer-1 and OccFormer, while label-free Mean-FLIP matches or outperforms supervised Taylor ranking on all five settings where that control was run. The method applies when removable primitives keep a stable checkpoint-row identity; it is budget-free in survivor count, not parameter-free.

Abstract:Egocentric human videos offer a scalable alternative to costly robot demonstra- tions, yet transferring their manipulation priors to robot control remains chal- lenging. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20881

作者：Utku Cicek,Mahip Singh,Ziyao Shang,Kimathi Kaai,C Thomas,Pedro Pablo Guerrero Vela,Alexander Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 19 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:While foundation models and vision transformers (ViTs) have redefined visual representations, current active learning (AL) strategies for these backbones largely operate at the patch or region level, where annotation requirements remain high due to extensive pixel labeling. Extending ViT-based AL to the pixel-level, low-budget regime is uniquely challenging: with as little as one pixel query per image, a method must simultaneously identify the most informative locations and train a reliable decoder head from an extremely sparse signal. We introduce TEAL (Token-space Efficient Active Learning), the first pixel-level AL framework built around ViT representations. TEAL uses a frozen ViT backbone and performs diversity-based selection directly on its native token lattice, avoiding the artifacts introduced by interpolating token embeddings to dense pixel resolutions. Across CamVid, Cityscapes, ADE20K, and Pascal Context, TEAL consistently outperforms previous baselines under extreme label scarcity, improving over the strongest baseline by up to +24.36 mIoU with a single labeled pixel (px) per image, and reaching up to 97% of fully supervised performance with just 10 px. To rigorously establish the source of the gains, we further show that the improvements persist when all baselines are equipped with the same DINOv3 backbone, demonstrating that coupling token-space selection with uncertainty refinement on the decoder grid makes ViT token geometry an effective foundation for extremely low-budget active segmentation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-to-video models often generate the visual concepts requested in natural language prompts but fail to satisfy their intended ordering, timing, and persistence requirements. We propose representing these requirements as specifications in a formal Temporal Logic, and introduce Temporal Specification Guidance (TSG), a training-free inference-time framework that turns temporal specifications into executable objectives for guiding video generation during sampling. Abstract:Unified multimodal generation covers discrete, continuous, and hybrid paradigms. Discrete approaches with single-codebook visual tokenizers align naturally with text modeling but have limited representational capacity. Continuous and hybrid approaches retain richer visual representations, but they model text and images in heterogeneous spaces with different training objectives, potentially introducing optimization conflicts between modalities with misaligned representation spaces. These choices expose a central question: how to efficiently model high-capacity visual representations alongside textual content in a shared optimization space within a single backbone. We present Intern Lumina U2, to our knowledge the first diffusion language model to represent multi-codebook visual tokens in a fully discrete space. At the input, the embeddings are concatenated at each position; at the output, a lightweight multi-codebook autoregressive head predicts the codes within each position sequentially, while the diffusion backbone processes all positions in parallel. Built on the unified 4D representation of AToken, Intern Lumina U2 supports image, video, and 3D inputs within a single backbone and achieves leading performance among unified models in visual understanding, image generation, and image editing. We further build a full-stack training, inference, and evaluation system on both Ascend and NVIDIA platforms to verify the cross-platform effectiveness of our model. We also improve the inference efficiency through a stability-aware sampling strategy, which combines confidence-plateau-guided unmasking approach with drift-triggered cache refreshing mechanisms, accelerating image generation by 4.45x.

153. 【2610.20761】Purify Before You Align: Improving Representation Alignment for Diffusion Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20761

作者：Yingheng Wang,Yaoqiang Li,Yaqin Wu,Junwen Bai,Jiatao Gu,Christopher De Sa,Volodymyr Kuleshov

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：encouraging intermediate features, pretrained representations, encouraging intermediate, intermediate features, features to match

备注： Code and checkpoints are on the project page: [this https URL](https://yingheng-wang.com/LAP)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Representation alignment improves and accelerates diffusion training by encouraging intermediate features to match a pretrained visual encoder. However, standard alignment matches pretrained representations without distinguishing what information may be more useful for generation. We introduce local appearance purification (LAP), which improves generation quality and makes encoders such as MAE more effective alignment targets by removing locally predictable content and aligning to the residual. LAP modifies only the alignment target, and requires no changes to diffusion training or inference. Through controlled interventions within a fixed encoder, we show that effective alignment depends on what information the target emphasizes, not simply how much image information it contains. Our theory gives a variational interpretation of standard alignment and, for fixed local predictors, a sum of token-wise information bounds conditioned on local appearance. To understand why purification benefits some encoders more than others, we derive an exact constant-readout test that measures how well the target can be matched without using the diffusion model's hidden state. LAP improves generation quality across five pretrained representations (MAE, AIMv2, CLIP, DINOv2, DINOv3), with the largest gain for MAE: on ImageNet-256 with SiT-B/2, it reduces FID from $53.1$ to $42.3$. Its gains persist with stronger alignment recipes and tokenizers. Computed before diffusion training, the constant-readout score predicts the benefit of purification (Spearman $\rho=0.94$ across 13 encoders).
