To characterize this emerging trajectory, we organize model capabilities into a four-level hierarchy and discover that higher-level tasks remain relatively underexplored despite model progress. To bridge this gap, we explore model performance on multi-step world state prediction, a high-level task that requires models to forecast successive future world states from multimodal observations and diverse references. Such a task requires models to possess entangled understanding and generation capabilities, as well as extensive world knowledge to translate human action sequences, physical transformations, and abstract visual reasoning into coherent visual outputs. We introduce WorldCast, a benchmark comprising 320 manually designed cases across eight categories, and evaluate 12 representative models. The conducted experiments yield three key insights: (a) advances in frontier models are rapidly pushing the boundaries of intelligent world modeling, with the GPT-Image-2.5 System scoring 72.8 and demonstrating early-stage visual state prediction capabilities on real-world tasks; (b) WorldCast reveals substantial gaps between models: even Nano Banana Pro reaches only 42.8 and the best open-source model scores 20.1, with deficits in handling complex context, entangled references, and reasoning-heavy tasks; (c) strong generative models effectively draw on the capabilities of their underlying language models. These findings highlight the need for multimodal generative models that are capable of dynamic planning and reasoning while also being deeply unified to faithfully simulate real-world states.

155. 【2610.20693】Orbia: Do Generated Videos Form a Consistent 3D World?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20693

作者：Dongyue Lu,Guian Fang,Hur Lim,Yonggang Wu,Yanrong Wang,Hongshan Chen,Shengju Qian,Yue Liao,Wei Chow,Lingdong Kong,Tianxin Huang,Yu Yang,Hongji Yang,Junchao Huang,Ao Liang,Yihao Wang,Zihan Wang,Rong Li,Hanlin Chen,Xin Wang,Wei Tsang Ooi,Mike Zheng Shou,Shuicheng Yan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：visually appealing frames, Video generators serve, depict a consistent, generators serve, realistic and visually

备注： Technical Report; 70 pages, 43 figures, 12 tables; Project page at [this https URL](https://orbia-bench.github.io/) Code at [this https URL](https://github.com/worldbench/Orbia) Dataset at [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Orbia/Orbia)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video generators serve as interactive worlds for exploration and simulation, yet realistic and visually appealing frames do not necessarily depict a consistent 3D world. Existing benchmarks assess selected geometric properties or use vision-language models to judge memory and consistency, leaving systematic 3D evaluation underexplored. We introduce Orbia, the first benchmark dedicated to a systematic and comprehensive evaluation of 3D consistency in interactive world models. Through controlled exploration and revisit, we organize the evaluation into three dimensions: input preservation, generated-content persistence, and 3D self-consistency. For input preservation, registered RGB-D references and annotated anchors support appearance, geometry, and spatial stability measurements. For generated-content persistence, we compare revisits to regions absent from the input image with their first generated observations to test whether models preserve what they create. For 3D self-consistency, cross-view depth and feature correspondence, regional scale agreement, and held-out Gaussian rendering test whether generated views describe the same 3D scene. Minute-long exploration and revisits further test long-term consistency. Our construction and annotation pipeline brings together public datasets, self-captured scenes, and simulation, yielding 720 instances across indoor and outdoor environments and six exploration-and-revisit protocols. Evaluation spans 27 text-, action-, and camera-conditioned models through native-interface adapters. Our Orbia benchmark provides a systematic basis for diagnosing 3D inconsistency and developing more persistent video world models.

156. 【2610.20684】Learning Topology-Aware Representations for Mechanics-Relevant TPMS Metamaterials

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20684

作者：Songlin Duan,Shuo Yang,Yujia Zheng,Tobin Filleter

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computational Engineering, Finance, and Science (cs.CE); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (stat.ML)

关键词：Periodic Minimal Surface, Triply Periodic Minimal, mechanical Triply Periodic, Minimal Surface, Triply Periodic

备注： 29 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Learning informative representations of mechanical Triply Periodic Minimal Surface (TPMS) metamaterials is important for mechanics analysis and structural design, yet existing methods have rarely explicitly supervised learned representations with information about global structural connectivity that is relevant to mechanical response. We propose a topology-aware representation learning framework that integrates persistent homology into a DMAE backbone to explicitly encode global structural connectivity. A persistence-guided masking strategy prioritizes patches associated with persistent topological features, while an auxiliary persistent-homology decoder encourages the latent representation to retain information about topological feature counts across connected components, tunnels, and voids. We evaluate the learned representations on two complementary TPMS datasets using family classification, mechanical-property classification, unsupervised clustering, and regression. Across the two datasets, our method demonstrates strong performance in both structural family and mechanical-property classification, improving family classification by up to 3.9% while achieving the best linear-probe performance. More notably, unsupervised property-clustering performance improves by up to 26.7% and 51.5% across the two datasets, while strong regression performance is retained with $R^2=0.993$. These results establish persistent topology as an effective inductive bias for representation learning of complex three-dimensional mechanical metamaterials.

157. 【2610.20651】owards automatic digital garment assembly

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20651

作者：Alejandro Rodriguez

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

关键词：Creating digital garments, existing two-dimensional patterns, two-dimensional patterns remains, Creating digital, time-consuming process

备注： 11 pages, 8 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Creating digital garments from existing two-dimensional patterns remains a time-consuming process. Although patterns are widely available in digital form, their assembly in garment CAD software involves manual definition of sewing operations and arrangement of the pattern panels for drape simulation. We present two learning-based approaches that automate both tasks using the 2D vector pattern as their sole input. Our automatic sewing model operates at the segment level, leveraging a Transformer encoder with multiple prediction heads. Our novel architecture supports many-to-many, ordered segment correspondences, as well as hems, overlapping seams needed for multilayer garments, and construction properties such as seam allowances and stitch types, required for the finished virtual garment but neglected by previous methods. A complementary panel-level model predicts body-region assignments, layers, and placement coordinates to initialize the drape simulation. We evaluate both models on a curated dataset of 4,035 professionally assembled garment instances. The sewing model exactly recovers all seam and hem topologies of 63.3\% of test garments, while the arrangement model meets the specified placement criteria for all panels in 66.2\% of test garments. We also integrate both methods into garment CAD software, demonstrating automatic assembly of successfully predicted garments.

158. 【2610.20636】FlowBudget: Training-Free Sampling-Budget Estimation for Flow Matching Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20636

作者：Zahra Dehghani,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：making inference cost, continuous flow-based generators, inference cost depend, making inference, requires numerical ODE

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sampling from continuous flow-based generators such as flow matching models requires numerical ODE or SDE integration, making inference cost depend on the number of function evaluations (NFEs). In practice, the sampling budget is inherited from prior work or selected through expensive FID-versus-NFE sweeps, using FID to assess image-generation quality, requiring sample generation and evaluation at multiple budgets. This makes sampling-budget selection dependent on repeated empirical evaluation. We therefore ask a complementary question to solver design and timestep allocation: for a fixed pretrained model and solver, what numerical resolution is sufficient? We introduce FLOWBUDGET, a training-free estimator of solver-relative sampling complexity. Using simulation-free interpolation probes of the learned dynamics, FLOWBUDGET normalizes the leading one-step discretization error by the motion, estimates its coefficient from interpolation probes, and maps a numerical tolerance to a step budget using the solver order. We derive estimators for numerical solvers in flow~matching sampling, namely Euler and Heun for ODE sampling and additive-noise Euler--Maruyama for SDE sampling, and connect the ODE coefficients to terminal integration error. Experiments on SiT, FlowDCN and Rectified Flow models, spanning flow-model architectures and formulations, test whether FLOWBUDGET can estimate the NFE budget without generating samples at multiple budgets or performing an FID-versus-NFE sweep. The code is available at this https URL

159. 【2610.20632】MeddeX: A Retrieval-Augmented LLaMA-3 System with Gemini Vision for Evidence-Backed Medical Report Analysis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20632

作者：Rajdeep Pandhare,Rohit Kundaikar,Yakub Kojar,Shreyansh Tiwari

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Modern healthcare generates, healthcare generates increasingly, generates increasingly complex, increasingly complex medical, Modern healthcare

备注： 6 pages , 3 figures, published in ijsrem

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Modern healthcare generates increasingly complex medical reports that challenge clinical professionals in terms of time and interpretive burden. Manual review processes are slow, error-prone, and difficult to scale. This paper presents MeddeX, a Medical Report Analyzer that combines a QLoRA fine-tuned LLaMA-3 (8B) model with Retrieval-Augmented Gen eration (RAG) and a multimodal Gemini Vision layer to deliver accurate, concise, and evidence-backed summaries of medical documents. The system handles text-native PDFs, scanned image based PDFs, and direct radiological images through an intelligent hybrid extraction pipeline that includes a novel garbage-text detection mechanism for corrupted and UTF-16-encoded doc uments. Beyond single-report QA, MeddeX introduces report scoped retrieval for precise contextual grounding, an automated longitudinal report comparison engine, and a self-healing backfill pipeline that lazily processes legacy documents on demand. A FastAPI backend, Supabase vector store, and this http URL fron tend complete the end-to-end architecture. Evaluation against the base LLaMA3-Med42-8B model demonstrates consistent improvements across ROUGE-1 (+9.2%), ROUGE-2 (+10.0%), ROUGE-L (+11.8%), BLEU (+71.4%), and BERTScore (+0.8%), validating the benefit of domain-specific fine-tuning. MeddeX represents a practical and robust step toward AI-assisted clinical decision support.

160. 【2610.20627】BD-HazardVLM: Probing Vision-Language Models for Latent Defensive-Driving Hazards in Bangladesh Road Scenes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20627

作者：Abdullah Al Maruf,Md. Sajedul Islam,Tanmoy Mridha,Irfan Hossain Bhuiyan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：Global South traffic, structured Global South, Global South, weakly structured Global, South traffic remains

备注： Accepted at the ECCV 2026 Workshop on Safe and Defensive Autonomous Driving (SDAD). 16 pages, 1 figure, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models are increasingly used for driving-scene understanding, yet their defensive reasoning under weakly structured Global South traffic remains unclear. We introduce BD-HazardVLM, a diagnostic benchmark of 500 Bangladesh road-scene images, comprising 375 from RSUD20K and 125 from TFP-BD, annotated for hazard presence, primary hazard category, risk timing, severity, defensive actions, visible evidence, and hallucination- and Western-road-bias traps. We evaluate Qwen2.5-VL-7B, InternVL3-8B, and MiniCPM-V-4.6 using a shared zero-shot task definition and structured output schema. Binary hazard-presence accuracy ranges from 88.0 percent to 89.2 percent, essentially matching the 89.0 percent majority-class baseline. Fine-grained reasoning is substantially weaker: category accuracy remains below 40 percent, risk-timing accuracy ranges from 1.6 percent to 12.4 percent, and exact defensive-action matching remains below 2 percent. Although InternVL3-8B achieves the highest severity accuracy at 61.2 percent, it has zero recall on the 58 high-severity cases. Qwen exhibits severe category mode collapse, while all models show weak latent-hazard anticipation and near-zero self-reported trap recognition. For the evaluated models, these findings show that coarse hazard recognition does not imply reliable fine-grained defensive-driving reasoning and motivate geographically diverse safety evaluation.

161. 【2610.20349】NeuroDETR: Multiscale Discrete Spatiotemporal Tokenization for Asynchronous EEG Event Detection

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20349

作者：Yaozhi Wen,Kun Zhao,Wei Wei,Haoyang Chen,Kangning Wang,Yi Sun,Shaowei Gu,Dingkun Liu,Shuang Qiu,Huiguang He

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：detection supports practical, supports practical brain-computer, practical brain-computer interfaces, Asynchronous EEG event, Asynchronous EEG

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Asynchronous EEG event detection supports practical brain-computer interfaces by identifying event classes and temporal boundaries directly from continuous recordings without predefined event boundaries at inference time. The primary challenge lies in extracting event-related patterns embedded in ongoing background activity. To address this, we propose \textbf{NeuroDETR}, a discrete spatiotemporal multiscale framework that combines structured tokenization with event-aware feature refinement. Multiscale temporal codebooks capture brief responses and sustained dynamics, while Spatial-Pyramid VQ hierarchically encodes spatial structure across EEG channels. These complementary representations form a compact event memory for jointly classifying events and estimating their onsets and offsets. Before tokenization, we apply FOCAL, an event-aware sparse attention module that uses predicted event presence to guide temporal feature refinement and spatial aggregation, emphasizing likely event periods while preserving context across the recording. Experiments on eight EEG datasets that span diverse paradigms demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. These findings provide a basis for the development of BCI systems that recognize meaningful neural activity during continuous interaction and monitoring.

162. 【2610.19957】Complementary Future Token Pruning for World Action Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19957

作者：Baekseung Kim,Junhoo Lee,Sohyeon Hong,Suhyun Jeon,Minkyu Kim,Seungyeon Kim,Donghoon Han,Nojun Kwak

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：World action models, jointly predict future, tokens incurs substantial, substantial inference latency, World action

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World action models (WAMs) jointly predict future visual states and robot actions, but processing dense future-frame tokens incurs substantial inference latency. We propose CF-Prune, a training-free framework for pruning future-frame tokens in pretrained WAMs. Our key insight is that future visual predictions support action generation and therefore need not preserve every visual detail required for high-fidelity video synthesis. CF-Prune combines instruction and action attention to identify task-relevant tokens during the initial denoising forward pass, even when future-frame and action representations remain highly noisy. An entropy-based phase identification mechanism further retains dense computation during precision phases to mitigate pruning-induced performance degradation. Experiments on RoboLab, RoboCasa, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate consistent inference speedups. At a 35% pruning ratio, CF-Prune achieves average speedups of 1.19-1.27x while improving task success rates. At a 50% pruning ratio, it achieves 1.29-1.33x average speedups on both simulation benchmarks, with overall success rates within 1.0 percentage point of the baseline.

163. 【2507.07297】MagiC: Evaluating Multimodal Cognition Toward Grounded Visual Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07297

作者：Chengfei Wu,Ronald Seoh,Bingxuan Li,Liqiang Zhang,Fengrong Han,Dan Goldwasser

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Recent advances, visual question answering, grounded visual reasoning, advances in large, led to impressive

备注： AACL 2026 Main Conference

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advances in large vision-language models have led to impressive performance in visual question answering and multimodal reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether these models genuinely perform grounded visual reasoning or rely on superficial patterns and dataset biases. In this work, we introduce MagiC, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate grounded multimodal cognition, assessing not only answer accuracy but also the quality of step-by-step reasoning and its alignment with relevant visual evidence. Our benchmark includes approximately 5,500 weakly supervised QA examples generated from strong model outputs and 900 human-curated examples with fine-grained annotations, including answers, rationales, and bounding box groundings. We evaluate 15 vision-language models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters across four dimensions: final answer correctness, reasoning validity, grounding fidelity, and self-correction ability. MagiC further includes diagnostic settings to probe model robustness under adversarial visual cues and assess their capacity for introspective error correction. We introduce new metrics such as MagiScore and StepSense, and provide comprehensive analyses that reveal key limitations and opportunities in current approaches to grounded visual reasoning.

164. 【2610.22258】Denoising Textures is a Different Ballgame Altogether!

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22258

作者：Saptarshi Mandal,Oindrila Haldar,Chirayata Bhattacharya,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula,Zhiwei Xu,Thrasyvoulos N. Pappas,Gaurav Sharma

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：human brain effortlessly, brain effortlessly deciphers, effortlessly deciphers colors, details remains challenging, human brain

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The human brain effortlessly deciphers colors and textures from visual stimuli and reconstructs the world around us, yet preserving such details remains challenging in image denoising. While significant progress has been made in generic image denoising, the problem of texture denoising has remained relatively under-explored. Fine details, such as intricate patterns and repetitive structures, often suffer from over-smoothing even in deep learning based approaches for denoising. In this paper, we analyze the behavior of state-of-the-art (SOTA) denoisers on texture datasets and show that performance improvements are possible only by fine-tuning the denoisers on the texture datasets. Surprisingly, fine-tuning on texture datasets result in the models catastrophically forgetting the original dataset. These findings highlight the need for specialized treatment of textures in denoising. Since denoising quality directly impacts downstream tasks such as generative modelling, we argue that dedicated texture aware denoisers are essential for faithful texture reconstruction and synthesis.

165. 【2610.21452】Full-Duplex Interaction with Speech, Expressions and Gestures

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21452

作者：Siyin Wang,Wenyi Yu,Terumi Chiba,Xianzhao Chen,Xiaohai Tian,Jun Zhang,Lu Lu,Chao Zhang

类目：Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Sound (cs.SD)

关键词：conversation requires coordinating, requires coordinating speech, conversation requires, requires coordinating, coordinating speech

备注： 37 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Human face-to-face conversation requires coordinating speech, gestures and expressions across both speaking and listening. We present ELLSA 2, an end-to-end full-duplex dialogue model that jointly optimizes speech interaction and streaming gesture-expression generation. An autoregressive, chunk-wise synthesizer combines pretrained acoustic features with distinct listening and speaking hidden states from the dialogue backbone and speech synthesizer, enabling continuous non-verbal generation even when the model is verbally silent. To translate dialogue understanding into vivid semantic gestures, we introduce contextual semantic calls and template guidance, separating whether to invoke an available semantic gesture from how to realize it. We also establish a curated data and evaluation pipeline for dialog-aware co-speech gesture generation and investigate reference-based multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as automatic judges. Experiments show that ELLSA 2 remains highly competitive with open-source full-duplex speech interaction models and outperforms cascaded baselines across dialog-aware gesture and expression metrics. Semantic template guidance further significantly improves semantic gesture quality. Strong system-level correlations with human judgments support these reference-based scores as useful preliminary metrics for full-duplex and semantic gesture assessment. Together, these results advance dialogue modeling from spoken responses toward coordinated multimodal interaction.

166. 【2610.21183】Monotone Piecewise-Linear Harmonization of Multitemporal Multispectral Surface Reflectance With Unsupervised Deep Multiview Pseudoinvariant Features Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21183

作者：Lino Garda Denaro,Chia-Hsiang Lin

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Canonical Correlation Analysis, exhibit systematic inconsistencies, generally performed independently, Generalized Canonical Correlation, surface reflectance analysis

备注： 15 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Surface reflectance products derived from optical satellite may exhibit systematic inconsistencies across acquisition dates, because atmospheric correction is generally performed independently for each image. Such residual biases distort the temporal behavior of invariant ground targets and reduce the reliability of multitemporal surface reflectance analysis. This study proposes Deep Multitemporal Generalized Canonical Correlation Analysis (DeepMGCCA), an unsupervised deep multiview pseudoinvariant feature learning framework that integrates Deep Generalized Canonical Correlation Analysis (GCCA) with Multivariate Alteration Detection (MAD) to identify deep-pseudoinvariant features (DIFs) for the removal of surface reflectance biases across multitemporal satellite observations. Unlike conventional MAD-based approaches that rely on linear GCCA, the proposed framework replaces date-specific linear projections with a shared convolutional encoder. The framework learns deep invariant features (DIFs) directly from pixel-weighted canonical variates via iterative reweighting strategy, while a reconstruction regularizer preserves surface reflectance information and prevents degenerate collapse from suppressing genuine temporal variability. Experiments were conducted on four annual Landsat-8/9 and Sentinel-2 acquisitions across four regions of interest (ROIs) to evaluate four DIF-based harmonization transforms. A monotone piecewise-linear transform derived from DIFs quantiles consistently achieved the largest bias reduction, lowering the between-date spectral angle by up to 2.54 and RMSE by 0.015, and raising PSNR by up to 4.04~dB. These findings demonstrate the potential of deep invariant feature learning for multitemporal surface reflectance bias removal while highlighting the importance of preserving physically meaningful temporal and spectral information during nonlinear representation learning.
